href="/docs/_favicons/light-192.png" sizes="192x192" type="image/png"/><link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/docs/_favicons/apple-touch-icon.png" sizes="180x180" type="image/png"/><style>#intro-video {
  aspect-ratio: 16/9;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 720px;
  height: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
}

@container (max-width: 720px) {
  #intro-video {
    width: 100%;
    margin: 1rem auto;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   Custom landing page (mode: frame)
   ============================================================ */

/* `mode: frame` gives the page the whole content area with no inset, so the
   landing starts where docs7 starts the article on other pages: the page
   gutter below lg (PAGE_GUTTER in docs7's renderer/lib/page-gutter.ts), then
   the article column's inset. Update these if docs7 changes them. */
.u-wrap {
  --u-inset: 1.5rem;
  box-sizing: content-box;
  max-width: 63.75rem;
  padding: 0 var(--u-inset) 6rem;
}
@media (width >= 40rem) {
  .u-wrap {
    --u-inset: 2.25rem;
  }
}
@media (width >= 48rem) {
  .u-wrap {
    --u-inset: 2.75rem;
  }
}
@media (width >= 64rem) {
  .u-wrap {
    --u-inset: var(--docs-column-gap);
  }
}
@media (width >= 80rem) {
  .u-wrap {
    --u-inset: calc(var(--docs-column-gap) + var(--docs-panel-inset));
  }
}

/* In `mode: frame` Mintlify keeps the left sidebar but STILL reserves a 28rem
   right column for the table of contents, which frame hides, leaving it empty and
   squishing the content. On the Get Started landing we collapse that empty column
   and let content use the full width. Scoped with Mintlify's documented
   data-current-path hook (only the /introduction page). NOTE: still targets
   Mintlify-internal ids (#content-area / #content-side-layout); revisit if a
   Mintlify upgrade renames them. Collapse to zero width, not display:none (which
   broke the sticky sidebar, making it render scrolled on load).
   2026-07: production's renderer no longer has #content-side-layout (rule is
   inert there, frame mode fills the width natively now), but local `mint dev`
   still renders it; keep until the CLI catches up. */
html[data-current-path="/introduction"] #content-side-layout {
  width: 0 !important;
  min-width: 0 !important;
  max-width: 0 !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
}
html[data-current-path="/introduction"] #content-area {
  width: 100% !important;
}

/* The landing body is client-rendered (imported MDX snippet components are not
   SSR'd), so on a hard load the content area is empty at first paint. That let
   the footer sit inside the viewport, where Mintlify's footer-avoidance —
   which sets a negative inline `top` on the fixed #sidebar and only re-runs on
   scroll — pinned the sidebar above the viewport; when the body then hydrated
   and the footer moved out of view, nothing re-ran the handler and the sidebar
   stayed stuck showing only the tail of the nav. Reserve a viewport of height
   from the first paint so the footer can never be in view while the body is
   still empty. Must be #content[data-page-href]: it is server-rendered with
   the real path, while html[data-current-path] SSRs as "/" and is only stamped
   after hydration - too late. Local `mint dev` stamps the attribute with its
   build path ("/src/_props/introduction"), hence the second selector. Don't
   collapse them into [data-page-href$="/introduction"]: that would also match
   pages like /redis/search/introduction. */
#content[data-page-href="/introduction"],
#content[data-page-href="/src/_props/introduction"] {
  min-height: 100vh;
}


/* ---- Hero ---- */
.u-hero {
  padding: 3.125rem 0 0;
}
.u-hero h1 {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  line-height: 1.2;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  margin: 0;
}
.u-hero p {
  margin: 0.5rem 0 0;
  max-width: 34.5rem;
  font-size: 1.125rem;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: #737373;
}
.dark .u-hero p {
  color: #a3a3a3;
}

/* Strong heading/title color. Mintlify's base content text is a muted gray
   (text-gray-500 / dark:text-gray-400) which washes out our large headings.
   Override so headings read crisp in both themes. */
.u-hero h1,
.u-section h2,
.u-card__title {
  color: #262626;
}
.dark .u-hero h1,
.dark .u-section h2,
.dark .u-card__title {
  color: #f4f5f6;
}

.u-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  margin-top: 1.5rem;
}
.u-action {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.625rem;
  height: 2.625rem;
  min-height: 2.625rem;
  padding: 0 1.25rem;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 2.5rem;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: 0.9375rem;
  line-height: 1;
  text-decoration: none;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
  transition:
    background 0.15s ease,
    border-color 0.15s ease,
    color 0.15s ease;
}
.u-action:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #10b981;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.u-action--primary {
  background: #262626;
  color: #fff;
}
.u-action--primary:hover {
  background: #27272a;
}
.dark .u-action--primary {
  background: #f4f4f5;
  color: #18181b;
}
.dark .u-action--primary:hover {
  background: #e4e4e7;
}
.u-prompt-action .prompt {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  background: transparent;
}
.u-prompt-action .prompt > div + div {
  display: none;
}
.u-prompt-action .prompt button {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 2;
  inset: 0;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  width: 13.125rem;
  height: 2.625rem;
  min-height: 2.625rem;
  padding: 0 1.25rem 0 6rem;
  border: 1px solid #e5e5e5;
  border-radius: 2.5rem;
  font-size: 0.9375rem;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1;
  color: #262626;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
}
.u-prompt-action .prompt button:hover {
  color: #262626;
  background: #fafafa;
  border-color: #d4d4d4;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
}
.dark .u-prompt-action .prompt button {
  color: #f4f4f5;
  background: transparent;
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}
.dark .u-prompt-action .prompt button:hover {
  color: #fff;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}
.u-prompt-action {
  position: relative;
  width: 13.125rem;
  height: 2.625rem;
}
.u-prompt-action .prompt button > span:has(svg) {
  display: none;
}
.u-prompt-tools {
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 3;
  top: 0.3125rem;
  left: 0.375rem;
  display: flex;
  width: 5rem;
  height: 2rem;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.u-prompt-tool {
  display: grid;
  flex: none;
  width: 2rem;
  height: 2rem;
  padding: 0.25rem;
  place-items: center;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  border-radius: 9999px;
  color: #262626;
  background: #fff;
}
.u-prompt-tool + .u-prompt-tool {
  margin-left: -0.5rem;
}
.u-prompt-tool svg {
  width: 1.375rem;
  height: 1.375rem;
}
.dark .u-prompt-tool {
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
  color: #f4f4f5;
  background: #18181b;
}

/* ---- Section headings ---- */
.u-section {
  margin-top: 4rem;
}
.u-section--divider {
  padding-top: 4rem;
  border-top: 1px solid #e5e5e5;
}
.dark .u-section--divider {
  border-top-color: #27272a;
}
.u-section h2 {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  line-height: 1.3;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  margin: 0;
}
/* Mintlify renders the sub as <p> or <span> depending on content, so target
   the class only and force block display. */
.u-section .u-sub {
  display: block;
  margin: 0.25rem 0 0;
  color: #737373;
  font-size: 0.9375rem;
  line-height: 1.7;
}
.dark .u-section .u-sub {
  color: #a3a3a3;
}
.u-section .u-sub a {
  color: inherit;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-decoration: underline;
  text-underline-offset: 2px;
}
.u-section .u-sub a:hover {
  color: #059669;
}
.dark .u-section .u-sub a:hover {
  color: #34d399;
}

/* ---- Grids ---- */
.u-grid {
  display: grid;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-top: 1.5rem;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(1, minmax(0, 1fr));
}
/* auto-fill so columns respond to the actual available width (works whether the
   page is full-bleed `custom` or narrowed by the `frame` sidebar). */
.u-grid--2 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(18rem, 1fr));
}
.u-grid--3 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(15rem, 1fr));
}
/* fixed-column variants (don't reflow to more columns on very wide layouts) */
.u-grid--fixed2 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
}
.u-grid--fixed3 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
}
.u-grid--fixed4 {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
}
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .u-grid--fixed3,
  .u-grid--fixed4 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}
@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .u-grid--fixed2,
  .u-grid--fixed3,
  .u-grid--fixed4 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

/* ---- Cards ---- */
.u-card {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.9rem;
  align-items: flex-start;
  padding: 1.1rem;
  border-radius: 0.9rem;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
  background: #fff;
  text-decoration: none;
  color: inherit;
  transition: border-color 0.15s ease;
}
.dark .u-card {
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
}
.u-card:hover {
  border-color: #d4d4d4;
}
.dark .u-card:hover {
  border-color: #52525b;
}
.u-card--static:hover {
  border-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
}
.dark .u-card--static:hover {
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}
.u-card__icon {
  flex: none;
  width: 2.25rem;
  height: 2.25rem;
  border-radius: 0.55rem;
  /* override Mintlify prose <img> margins that otherwise inflate the card and
     push the icon off the top */
  margin: 0 !important;
  align-self: flex-start;
  /* let clicks pass through to the card link instead of triggering Mintlify's
     image zoom */
  pointer-events: none;
}
.u-card__icon--muted {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--primary);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--primary) 10%, transparent);
}
.dark .u-card__icon--muted {
  color: var(--primary-light);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--primary-light) 10%, transparent);
}
.u-card__lang-icon {
  width: 1.5rem;
  height: 1.5rem;
}
.u-card__icon--brand {
  width: 2rem;
  height: 2rem;
  border-radius: 0;
  align-self: center;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: none;
}
.u-card__body {
  min-width: 0;
}
.u-card__title {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  line-height: 1.3;
}
.u-card__desc {
  margin-top: 0.2rem;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: #626d7a;
}
.dark .u-card__desc {
  color: #aab2bf;
}

/* ---- Product cards (larger) ---- */
.u-product .u-card__icon {
  width: 2rem;
  height: 2rem;
  border-radius: 0;
  align-self: center;
}
.u-product,
.u-tool,
.u-community {
  min-height: 5.125rem;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1rem;
  padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
  border: 1px solid #e5e5e5;
  border-radius: 1.25rem;
  background: #fafafa;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
}
.u-product .u-card__title,
.u-tool .u-card__title,
.u-community .u-card__title {
  font-size: 1.0625rem;
  line-height: 1.3;
  font-weight: 600;
}
.u-product .u-card__desc,
.u-tool .u-card__desc,
.u-community .u-card__desc {
  margin-top: 0;
  font-size: 0.8125rem;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: #737373;
}
.dark .u-product,
.dark .u-tool,
.dark .u-community {
  border-color: #3f3f46;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}
.dark .u-product .u-card__desc,
.dark .u-tool .u-card__desc,
.dark .u-community .u-card__desc {
  color: #a3a3a3;
}

/* ---- Console showcase ---- */
.u-console {
  margin-top: 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #f7f8fa;
}
.dark .u-console {
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
}
.u-console img {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 13rem;
  object-fit: cover;
  object-position: top center;
}
.u-console__cap {
  padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  color: #626d7a;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
}
.dark .u-console__cap {
  color: #98a2b3;
  border-top-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

/* ---- Agent grid (agent-resources/overview) ---- */
.u-agents {
  margin: 1.5rem 0 2rem;
}
.u-card--stack {
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.55rem;
  padding: 1rem;
}
.u-card__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem;
}
.u-card__brand-icon {
  width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.25rem;
  flex: none;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  color: #18181b;
}
.dark .u-card__brand-icon {
  color: #fafafa;
}
.u-card__brand-icon svg {
  width: 1.25rem;
  height: 1.25rem;
}
/* Mintlify adds a `.link` class (green border-bottom) and prose colors to every
   anchor on regular pages; override both for the card. */
.u-card--stack {
  color: #111827 !important;
  text-decoration: none !important;
  border-bottom-width: 1px !important;
}
.dark .u-card--stack {
  color: #f9fafb !important;
}
/* always exactly 3 lines tall; longer text is clamped with an ellipsis */
.u-card--stack .u-card__desc {
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  line-height: 1.45;
  height: calc(0.85rem * 1.45 * 3);
  display: -webkit-box;
  -webkit-line-clamp: 3;
  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
  overflow: hidden;
}

/* ---- Agent setup card on product landing pages (snippets/agent-setup.jsx) ---- */
.u-agent-setup {
  margin: 0 0 1.5rem;
  align-items: center;
}

prose-headings:tracking-[-0.01em] prose-h1:mt-12 prose-h1:text-2xl prose-h2:text-xl prose-h3:text-lg" data-page-title="Upstash Redis Getting Started" data-page-href="/docs/redis/overall/getstarted"><a class="u-card u-agent-setup" href="/docs/agent-resources/overview"><div class="u-card__icon u-card__icon--muted"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 8V4H8"></path><rect width="16" height="12" x="4" y="8" rx="2"></rect><path d="M2 14h2"></path><path d="M20 14h2"></path><path d="M15 13v2"></path><path d="M9 13v2"></path></svg></div><div class="u-card__body"><div class="u-card__title">Building with an AI coding agent?</div><div class="u-card__desc">Connect the Upstash MCP server and the Redis skill to Claude Code, Cursor, Codex, and other agents.</div></div></a>
<p>Upstash Redis is a <strong>highly available, infinitely scalable</strong> Redis-compatible database:</p>
<ul>
<li>99.99% uptime guarantee with auto-scaling (<a class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)" href="/docs/redis/overall/enterprise#prod-pack-features">Prod Pack</a>)</li>
<li>Ultra-low latency worldwide</li>
<li>Multi-region replication</li>
<li>Multi-Zone High Availability (<a class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)" href="/docs/redis/overall/enterprise#prod-pack-features">Prod Pack</a>)</li>
<li>Durable, persistent storage without sacrificing performance</li>
<li>Automatic backups</li>
<li>Optional SOC-2 compliance, encryption at rest and much more</li>
</ul>
<div role="note" data-callout-type="tip" class="callout my-5 flex min-w-0 gap-3 rounded-[20px] border p-5 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] border-emerald-200 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-emerald-50),var(--surface-raised))] dark:border-emerald-900 dark:bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-emerald-950),var(--surface-raised))]"><span aria-hidden="true" data-component-part="callout-icon" class="callout-icon flex w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-5 h-[18.2px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.75" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="text-[13px] font-medium leading-[1.4] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">Tip</div><div data-component-part="callout-content" class="min-w-0 break-words text-[15px] leading-[1.5] text-(--text-secondary) mt-1.5 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0 [&amp;&gt;p+p]:mt-2"><p>If you are an AI agent and need a Redis DB to use or experiment with, send a POST request to <a href="https://upstash.com/start-redis" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)">https://upstash.com/start-redis</a>. Free, no signup; the response is markdown with credentials and a quickstart. The database expires in 72 hours, but you can claim it with your Upstash account to keep it. Especially useful for AI agents that need scratch storage on the fly. If you are an agent, always send a <code>User-Agent</code> header identifying which agent you are (e.g. <code>claude-code</code>, <code>cursor</code>, <code>codex</code>, <code>opencode</code>) on every request you make to this API.</p></div></div></div>
<hr/>
<h2 id="1-create-an-upstash-redis-database" class="group scroll-mt-28">1. Create an Upstash Redis Database<a href="#1-create-an-upstash-redis-database" aria-label="Link to this section" class="ml-2 text-(--text-secondary) no-underline opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">#</a></h2>
<p>Log in to the <a href="https://console.upstash.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)">Upstash Console</a> (or <a href="https://console.upstash.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)">sign up</a> for a free account). From the <strong>Redis</strong> tab, click <code>+ Create Database</code> in the upper right corner. A dialog opens up:</p>
<figure class="frame not-prose my-5 rounded-2xl bg-(--surface-sunken) p-1"><div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-(--surface-raised) p-1 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(25,28,33,0.04),0_1px_2px_rgba(25,28,33,0.06),0_0_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] dark:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.01),0_0_0_1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.4),0_0_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)]"><div aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(10,10,10,0.1)_1px,transparent_1px)] dark:bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(255,255,255,0.06)_1px,transparent_1px)] [background-size:14px_14px] [mask-image:radial-gradient(85%_120%_at_50%_42%,black,transparent)]"></div><div class="relative flex justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg [&amp;_iframe]:aspect-video [&amp;_iframe]:h-auto [&amp;_iframe]:w-full [&amp;_img]:my-0 [&amp;_img]:w-full [&amp;_img]:rounded-lg"><img width="1518" height="977" data-docs-image-width="1518" data-docs-image-height="977" loading="lazy" decoding="async" alt="Create a Global Redis database" sizes="auto" src="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1518/img/getting_started/create-global.png.webp" srcSet="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/640/img/getting_started/create-global.png.webp 640w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/960/img/getting_started/create-global.png.webp 960w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1280/img/getting_started/create-global.png.webp 1280w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1518/img/getting_started/create-global.png.webp 1518w"/></div></div></figure>
<p><strong>Database Name:</strong> Enter a name for your database.</p>
<p><strong>Primary Region and Read Regions:</strong> For optimal performance, select the Primary Region closest to where most of your writes will occur. Select the read region(s) where most of your reads will occur.</p>
<p>Once you click <code>Next</code> and select a plan, your database is running and ready to connect:</p>
<figure class="frame not-prose my-5 rounded-2xl bg-(--surface-sunken) p-1"><div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-(--surface-raised) p-1 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(25,28,33,0.04),0_1px_2px_rgba(25,28,33,0.06),0_0_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] dark:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.01),0_0_0_1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.4),0_0_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)]"><div aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(10,10,10,0.1)_1px,transparent_1px)] dark:bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(255,255,255,0.06)_1px,transparent_1px)] [background-size:14px_14px] [mask-image:radial-gradient(85%_120%_at_50%_42%,black,transparent)]"></div><div class="relative flex justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg [&amp;_iframe]:aspect-video [&amp;_iframe]:h-auto [&amp;_iframe]:w-full [&amp;_img]:my-0 [&amp;_img]:w-full [&amp;_img]:rounded-lg"><img width="100%" data-docs-image-width="1000" data-docs-image-height="715" loading="lazy" decoding="async" alt="Redis database details in the Upstash Console" style="--docs-image-aspect-ratio:auto 1000 / 715" sizes="auto" src="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1000/img/getting_started/database.png.webp" srcSet="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/640/img/getting_started/database.png.webp 640w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/960/img/getting_started/database.png.webp 960w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1000/img/getting_started/database.png.webp 1000w"/></div></div></figure>
<hr/>
<h2 id="2-connect-to-your-database" class="group scroll-mt-28">2. Connect to Your Database<a href="#2-connect-to-your-database" aria-label="Link to this section" class="ml-2 text-(--text-secondary) no-underline opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">#</a></h2>
<p>You can connect to Upstash Redis with any Redis client. For simplicity, we’ll use <code>redis-cli</code>. See the <a href="../howto/connect-client" class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)">Connect Your Client</a> section for connecting via our TypeScript or Python SDKs and other clients.</p>
<div role="note" data-callout-type="tip" class="callout my-5 flex min-w-0 gap-3 rounded-[20px] border p-5 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] border-emerald-200 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-emerald-50),var(--surface-raised))] dark:border-emerald-900 dark:bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-emerald-950),var(--surface-raised))]"><span aria-hidden="true" data-component-part="callout-icon" class="callout-icon flex w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-5 h-[18.2px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.75" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5"></path><path d="M9 18h6"></path><path d="M10 22h4"></path></svg></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="text-[13px] font-medium leading-[1.4] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">Tip</div><div data-component-part="callout-content" class="min-w-0 break-words text-[15px] leading-[1.5] text-(--text-secondary) mt-1.5 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0 [&amp;&gt;p+p]:mt-2"><p>You can copy the <code>redis-cli</code> command to connect to your database from the <strong>Details</strong> tab of your database in the Upstash Console. The <strong>Token</strong> shown there is also your database’s password.</p></div></div></div>
<p>The Redis CLI is included in the official Redis distribution. If you don’t
have Redis installed, you can get it <a href="https://redis.io/docs/latest/operate/oss_and_stack/install/install-redis/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)">here</a>.</p>
<p>Connect to your database and execute commands on it:</p>
<div class="code-block code-frame not-prose group -mx-1 rounded-2xl bg-(--surface-sunken) p-1"><div class="code-surface overflow-hidden rounded-xl shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(25,28,33,0.04),0_1px_2px_rgba(25,28,33,0.06),0_0_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] <p>As you run commands, you’ll see updates to your database metrics in (almost) real-time. These database metrics are refreshed every 10 seconds.</p>
<figure class="frame not-prose my-5 rounded-2xl bg-(--surface-sunken) p-1"><div class="relative overflow-hidden rounded-xl bg-(--surface-raised) p-1 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgba(25,28,33,0.04),0_1px_2px_rgba(25,28,33,0.06),0_0_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] dark:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.01),0_0_0_1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2),0_1px_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.4),0_0_3px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)]"><div aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(10,10,10,0.1)_1px,transparent_1px)] dark:bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(255,255,255,0.06)_1px,transparent_1px)] [background-size:14px_14px] [mask-image:radial-gradient(85%_120%_at_50%_42%,black,transparent)]"></div><div class="relative flex justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg [&amp;_iframe]:aspect-video [&amp;_iframe]:h-auto [&amp;_iframe]:w-full [&amp;_img]:my-0 [&amp;_img]:w-full [&amp;_img]:rounded-lg"><img width="100%" data-docs-image-width="1000" data-docs-image-height="190" loading="lazy" decoding="async" alt="Redis metrics charts in the Upstash Console" style="--docs-image-aspect-ratio:auto 1000 / 190" sizes="auto" src="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1000/img/getting_started/charts.png.webp" srcSet="/docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/640/img/getting_started/charts.png.webp 640w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/960/img/getting_started/charts.png.webp 960w, /docs/docs-assets/_docs7/i/1000/img/getting_started/charts.png.webp 1000w"/></div></div></figure>
<p>Congratulations! You have created an ultra-fast Upstash Redis database! 🎉</p>
<div role="note" data-callout-type="check" class="callout my-5 flex min-w-0 gap-3 rounded-[20px] border p-5 shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.06)] border-green-200 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-green-50),var(--surface-raised))] dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,var(--color-green-950),var(--surface-raised))]"><span aria-hidden="true" data-component-part="callout-icon" class="callout-icon flex w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center [&amp;&gt;svg]:size-5 h-[18.2px] text-green-700 dark:text-green-400"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.75" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="m9 12 2 2 4-4"></path></svg></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><div class="text-[13px] font-medium leading-[1.4] text-green-700 dark:text-green-400">Check</div><div data-component-part="callout-content" class="min-w-0 break-words text-[15px] leading-[1.5] text-(--text-secondary) mt-1.5 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0 [&amp;&gt;p+p]:mt-2"><p><strong>New: Manage Upstash Redis with your agent</strong></p><p>Manage Upstash Redis databases from Claude and other AI tools by using our <a class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)" href="/docs/agent-resources/mcp">MCP server</a>. To let an agent run commands directly against a single database, use the dedicated <a class="font-medium text-(--text-primary) underline decoration-(--border-hover) underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-(--text-muted)" href="/docs/redis/sdks/mcp">Redis MCP</a>.</p></div></div></div>

are an AI agent and need a Redis DB to use or experiment with, send a POST request to https://upstash.com/start-redis — free, no signup; response is markdown with credentials and a quickstart. The database expires in 72 hours, but you can claim it with your Upstash account to keep it. Especially useful for AI agents that need scratch storage on the fly. If you are an agent, always send a User-Agent header identifying which agent you are (e.g. claude-code, cursor, codex, opencode) on every request you make to this API." class="max-w-[28rem] text-sm text-(--text-secondary) hover:text-(--text-primary) line-clamp-3">If you are an AI agent and need a Redis DB to use or experiment with, send a POST request to https://upstash.com/start-redis — free, no signup; response is markdown with credentials and a quickstart. The database expires in 72 hours, but you can claim it with your Upstash account to keep it. Especially useful for AI agents that need scratch storage on the fly. width: 100%;\n  max-width: 720px;\n  height: auto;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  margin: 2rem auto;\n}\n\n@container (max-width: 720px) {\n  #intro-video {\n    width: 100%;\n    margin: 1rem auto;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================\n   Custom landing page (mode: frame)\n   ============================================================ */\n\n/* `mode: frame` gives the page the whole content area with no inset, so the\n   landing starts where docs7 starts the article on other pages: the page\n   gutter below lg (PAGE_GUTTER in docs7's renderer/lib/page-gutter.ts), then\n   the article column's inset. Update these if docs7 changes them. */\n.u-wrap {\n  --u-inset: 1.5rem;\n  box-sizing: content-box;\n  max-width: 63.75rem;\n  padding: 0 var(--u-inset) 6rem;\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 40rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: 2.25rem;\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 48rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: 2.75rem;\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 64rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: var(--docs-column-gap);\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 80rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: calc(var(--docs-column-gap) + var(--docs-panel-inset));\n  }\n}\n\n/* In `mode: frame` Mintlify keeps the left sidebar but STILL reserves a 28rem\n   right column for the table of contents, which frame hides, leaving it empty and\n   squishing the content. On the Get Started landing we collapse that empty column\n   and let content use the full width. Scoped with Mintlify's documented\n   data-current-path hook (only the /introduction page). NOTE: still targets\n   Mintlify-internal ids (#content-area / #content-side-layout); revisit if a\n   Mintlify upgrade renames them. Collapse to zero width, not display:none (which\n   broke the sticky sidebar, making it render scrolled on load).\n   2026-07: production's renderer no longer has #content-side-layout (rule is\n   inert there, frame mode fills the width natively now), but local `mint dev`\n   still renders it; keep until the CLI catches up. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/introduction\"] #content-side-layout {\n  width: 0 !important;\n  min-width: 0 !important;\n  max-width: 0 !important;\n  overflow: hidden !important;\n}\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/introduction\"] #content-area {\n  width: 100% !important;\n}\n\n/* The landing body is client-rendered (imported MDX snippet components are not\n   SSR'd), so on a hard load the content area is empty at first paint. That let\n   the footer sit inside the viewport, where Mintlify's footer-avoidance —\n   which sets a negative inline `top` on the fixed #sidebar and only re-runs on\n   scroll — pinned the sidebar above the viewport; when the body then hydrated\n   and the footer moved out of view, nothing re-ran the handler and the sidebar\n   stayed stuck showing only the tail of the nav. Reserve a viewport of height\n   from the first paint so the footer can never be in view while the body is\n   still empty. Must be #content[data-page-href]: it is server-rendered with\n   the real path, while html[data-current-path] SSRs as \"/\" and is only stamped\n   after hydration - too late. Local `mint dev` stamps the attribute with its\n   build path (\"/src/_props/introduction\"), hence the second selector. Don't\n   collapse them into [data-page-href$=\"/introduction\"]: that would also match\n   pages like /redis/search/introduction. */\n#content[data-page-href=\"/introduction\"],\n#content[data-page-href=\"/src/_props/introduction\"] {\n  min-height: 100vh;\n}\n\n\n/* ---- Hero ---- */\n.u-hero {\n  padding: 3.125rem 0 0;\n}\n.u-hero h1 {\n  font-size: 2.5rem;\n  line-height: 1.2;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  letter-spacing: 0;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n.u-hero p {\n  margin: 0.5rem 0 0;\n  max-width: 34.5rem;\n  font-size: 1.125rem;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n  color: #737373;\n}\n.dark .u-hero p {\n  color: #a3a3a3;\n}\n\n/* Strong heading/title color. Mintlify's base content text is a muted gray\n   (text-gray-500 / dark:text-gray-400) which washes out our large headings.\n   Override so headings read crisp in both themes. */\n.u-hero h1,\n.u-section h2,\n.u-card__title {\n  color: #262626;\n}\n.dark .u-hero h1,\n.dark .u-section h2,\n.dark .u-card__title {\n  color: #f4f5f6;\n}\n\n.u-actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  margin-top: 1.5rem;\n}\n.u-action {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 0.625rem;\n  height: 2.625rem;\n  min-height: 2.625rem;\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  border-radius: 2.5rem;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\n  line-height: 1;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n  transition:\n    background 0.15s ease,\n    border-color 0.15s ease,\n    color 0.15s ease;\n}\n.u-action:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #10b981;\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n.u-action--primary {\n  background: #262626;\n  color: #fff;\n}\n.u-action--primary:hover {\n  background: #27272a;\n}\n.dark .u-action--primary {\n  background: #f4f4f5;\n  color: #18181b;\n}\n.dark .u-action--primary:hover {\n  background: #e4e4e7;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 100%;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  background: transparent;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt \u003e div + div {\n  display: none;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt button {\n  position: absolute;\n  z-index: 2;\n  inset: 0;\n  justify-content: flex-end;\n  width: 13.125rem;\n  height: 2.625rem;\n  min-height: 2.625rem;\n  padding: 0 1.25rem 0 6rem;\n  border: 1px solid #e5e5e5;\n  border-radius: 2.5rem;\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  line-height: 1;\n  color: #262626;\n  background: #fff;\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt button:hover {\n  color: #262626;\n  background: #fafafa;\n  border-color: #d4d4d4;\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n}\n.dark .u-prompt-action .prompt button {\n  color: #f4f4f5;\n  background: transparent;\n  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);\n}\n.dark .u-prompt-action .prompt button:hover {\n  color: #fff;\n  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);\n  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);\n}\n.u-prompt-action {\n  position: relative;\n  width: 13.125rem;\n  height: 2.625rem;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt button \u003e span:has(svg) {\n  display: none;\n}\n.u-prompt-tools {\n  position: absolute;\n  z-index: 3;\n  top: 0.3125rem;\n  left: 0.375rem;\n  display: flex;\n  width: 5rem;\n  height: 2rem;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n.u-prompt-tool {\n  display: grid;\n  flex: none;\n  width: 2rem;\n  height: 2rem;\n  padding: 0.25rem;\n  place-items: center;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);\n  border-radius: 9999px;\n  color: #262626;\n  background: #fff;\n}\n.u-prompt-tool + .u-prompt-tool {\n  margin-left: -0.5rem;\n}\n.u-prompt-tool svg {\n  width: 1.375rem;\n  height: 1.375rem;\n}\n.dark .u-prompt-tool {\n  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);\n  color: #f4f4f5;\n  background: #18181b;\n}\n\n/* ---- Section headings ---- */\n.u-section {\n  margin-top: 4rem;\n}\n.u-section--divider {\n  padding-top: 4rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid #e5e5e5;\n}\n.dark .u-section--divider {\n  border-top-color: #27272a;\n}\n.u-section h2 {\n  font-size: 1.5rem;\n  line-height: 1.3;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  letter-spacing: 0;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n/* Mintlify renders the sub as \u003cp\u003e or \u003cspan\u003e depending on content, so target\n   the class only and force block display. */\n.u-section .u-sub {\n  display: block;\n  margin: 0.25rem 0 0;\n  color: #737373;\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n}\n.dark .u-section .u-sub {\n  color: #a3a3a3;\n}\n.u-section .u-sub a {\n  color: inherit;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  text-decoration: underline;\n  text-underline-offset: 2px;\n}\n.u-section .u-sub a:hover {\n  color: #059669;\n}\n.dark .u-section .u-sub a:hover {\n  color: #34d399;\n}\n\n/* ---- Grids ---- */\n.u-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  gap: 1rem;\n  margin-top: 1.5rem;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(1, minmax(0, 1fr));\n}\n/* auto-fill so columns respond to the actual available width (works whether the\n   page is full-bleed `custom` or narrowed by the `frame` sidebar). */\n.u-grid--2 {\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(18rem, 1fr));\n}\n.u-grid--3 {\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(15rem, 1fr));\n}\n/* fixed-column variants (don't reflow to more columns on very wide layouts) */\n.u-grid--fixed2 {\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\n}\n.u-grid--fixed3 {\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\n}\n.u-grid--fixed4 {\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));\n}\n@media (max-width: 900px) {\n  .u-grid--fixed3,\n  .u-grid--fixed4 {\n    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  }\n}\n@media aspect-ratio: 16/9;\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 720px;\n  height: auto;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  margin: 2rem auto;\n}\n\n@container (max-width: 720px) {\n  #intro-video {\n    width: 100%;\n    margin: 1rem auto;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================\n   Custom landing page (mode: frame)\n   ============================================================ */\n\n/* `mode: frame` gives the page the whole content area with no inset, so the\n   landing starts where docs7 starts the article on other pages: the page\n   gutter below lg (PAGE_GUTTER in docs7's renderer/lib/page-gutter.ts), then\n   the article column's inset. Update these if docs7 changes them. */\n.u-wrap {\n  --u-inset: 1.5rem;\n  box-sizing: content-box;\n  max-width: 63.75rem;\n  padding: 0 var(--u-inset) 6rem;\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 40rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: 2.25rem;\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 48rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: 2.75rem;\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 64rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: var(--docs-column-gap);\n  }\n}\n@media (width \u003e= 80rem) {\n  .u-wrap {\n    --u-inset: calc(var(--docs-column-gap) + var(--docs-panel-inset));\n  }\n}\n\n/* In `mode: frame` Mintlify keeps the left sidebar but STILL reserves a 28rem\n   right column for the table of contents, which frame hides, leaving it empty and\n   squishing the content. On the Get Started landing we collapse that empty column\n   and let content use the full width. Scoped with Mintlify's documented\n   data-current-path hook (only the /introduction page). NOTE: still targets\n   Mintlify-internal ids (#content-area / #content-side-layout); revisit if a\n   Mintlify upgrade renames them. Collapse to zero width, not display:none (which\n   broke the sticky sidebar, making it render scrolled on load).\n   2026-07: production's renderer no longer has #content-side-layout (rule is\n   inert there, frame mode fills the width natively now), but local `mint dev`\n   still renders it; keep until the CLI catches up. */\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/introduction\"] #content-side-layout {\n  width: 0 !important;\n  min-width: 0 !important;\n  max-width: 0 !important;\n  overflow: hidden !important;\n}\nhtml[data-current-path=\"/introduction\"] #content-area {\n  width: 100% !important;\n}\n\n/* The landing body is client-rendered (imported MDX snippet components are not\n   SSR'd), so on a hard load the content area is empty at first paint. That let\n   the footer sit inside the viewport, where Mintlify's footer-avoidance —\n   which sets a negative inline `top` on the fixed #sidebar and only re-runs on\n   scroll — pinned the sidebar above the viewport; when the body then hydrated\n   and the footer moved out of view, nothing re-ran the handler and the sidebar\n   stayed stuck showing only the tail of the nav. Reserve a viewport of height\n   from the first paint so the footer can never be in view while the body is\n   still empty. Must be #content[data-page-href]: it is server-rendered with\n   the real path, while html[data-current-path] SSRs as \"/\" and is only stamped\n   after hydration - too late. Local `mint dev` stamps the attribute with its\n   build path (\"/src/_props/introduction\"), hence the second selector. Don't\n   collapse them into [data-page-href$=\"/introduction\"]: that would also match\n   pages like /redis/search/introduction. */\n#content[data-page-href=\"/introduction\"],\n#content[data-page-href=\"/src/_props/introduction\"] {\n  min-height: 100vh;\n}\n\n\n/* ---- Hero ---- */\n.u-hero {\n  padding: 3.125rem 0 0;\n}\n.u-hero h1 {\n  font-size: 2.5rem;\n  line-height: 1.2;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  letter-spacing: 0;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n.u-hero p {\n  margin: 0.5rem 0 0;\n  max-width: 34.5rem;\n  font-size: 1.125rem;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n  color: #737373;\n}\n.dark .u-hero p {\n  color: #a3a3a3;\n}\n\n/* Strong heading/title color. Mintlify's base content text is a muted gray\n   (text-gray-500 / dark:text-gray-400) which washes out our large headings.\n   Override so headings read crisp in both themes. */\n.u-hero h1,\n.u-section h2,\n.u-card__title {\n  color: #262626;\n}\n.dark .u-hero h1,\n.dark .u-section h2,\n.dark .u-card__title {\n  color: #f4f5f6;\n}\n\n.u-actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  margin-top: 1.5rem;\n}\n.u-action {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 0.625rem;\n  height: 2.625rem;\n  min-height: 2.625rem;\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  border-radius: 2.5rem;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  font-weight: 400;\n  font-size: 0.9375rem;\n  line-height: 1;\n  text-decoration: none;\n  box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);\n  transition:\n    background 0.15s ease,\n    border-color 0.15s ease,\n    color 0.15s ease;\n}\n.u-action:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #10b981;\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n.u-action--primary {\n  background: #262626;\n  color: #fff;\n}\n.u-action--primary:hover {\n  background: #27272a;\n}\n.dark .u-action--primary {\n  background: #f4f4f5;\n  color: #18181b;\n}\n.dark .u-action--primary:hover {\n  background: #e4e4e7;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt {\n  position: absolute;\n  inset: 0;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 100%;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  background: transparent;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt \u003e div + div {\n  display: none;\n}\n.u-prompt-action .prompt button {\n  position: absolute;\n  z-index: 2;\n  inset: 0;\n  justify-content: flex-end;\n  width: 13.125rem;\n  height: 2.625rem;\n  min-height: 2.625rem;\n  padding: 0 1.25rem 0 6rem;\n  border: 1px solid #e5e5e5;\n  border-radius: the commands yourself instead of asking me to.\n\n1. Run `npx -y upstash setup --yes` with the flag for the agent you are (`--claude`, `--codex`, `--cursor`, `--gemini`, `--vscode`, `--copilot` or `--opencode`). It installs the Upstash plugin where your agent supports plugins, and otherwise adds the remote MCP server https://mcp.upstash.com/mcp (OAuth on first use) and the Upstash skills.\n2. For any other agent, install the skills with `npx -y skills add upstash/skills --global` and add https://mcp.upstash.com/mcp to your MCP config (see https://upstash.com/docs/agent-resources/clients).\n3. Tell me what was installed and whether I need to restart or reload (in Claude Code, `/reload-plugins`).\n\nThe skills cover:\n\n- SDKs for Redis, Ratelimit, QStash, Workflow, Vector, Search, Blob and Box\n- The `upstash` CLI\n- Running work in Box cloud sandboxes\n\nIf I need a Redis database before I have an account, POST https://upstash.com/start-redis for a free one with no signup (it lasts 3 days unless I claim it).\n\nFor anything else, read the docs at https://upstash.com/docs/introduction (every page is listed in
